# Trainer guide — Pokémon Heartfelt Gold

These tables are generated from the current hg-engine authoring source supplied for Heartfelt Gold. The patch binary has not been independently decoded against these tables. Encounter availability still depends on game progress.

Search by trainer name, location or internal ID. A blank location means the location catalog does not resolve that party variant; it does not mean the battle is unavailable. Moves and held items reflect the configured party.

Sources: [trainer parties](../../HG-Engine/data/Trainers.c), local `Data/Trainer/HG-Trainer-Locations.csv`.
